planetGen · version map 8.1 to 10.0, Core lanes 1 to 5 and Bug Hunt · updated 2026-10-30 12:41 AM EDT
Docs index · Version plan

Execution Plan

751 open items, 69 of them bugs. Version 8.1 (the infinite zoom map and the new generate scatters, halo and satellite galaxies included) is the headline in progress; its stamp comes when every item of its six phases is done. Five Core lanes and a bug lane build it in parallel, each through its own gate; the TODO lane keeps this page, the plan documents, the number map and the GitHub issues true, and every item ID below links to the issue that groups it with the other items of its goal.

Where each lane is

Updated 2026-10-30 12:41 AM EDT from the lanes' own status lines (docs/plan/lanes.md); times are the lanes' clocks as reported. A gate is the scripts/test.py changed run that has to be green before a lane's PR merges (Boss: merges need no human review).

Headline features and what each needs

Boss, 2026-10-10: every release has one headline feature. The open items each one needs, in build order (generated from docs/TODO.md; the TODO tree, version plan and TODO reference carry the same panel).

8.1 the new generate scatters: accurate galactic generation and scattering, including the halo (18 open items needed, 10 ready to start)
  1. GEN.744 Alpha-enhanced stellar tracks and an age-metallicity relation from the galaxy's own history 8.1 phase 11 · ready
  2. GEN.753 Validation sweep for each physics area: compare generated populations with published statistics 8.1 phase 11 · ready
  3. GEN.964 H-He equation of state tables (a): a setup catalogue entry and a reader with tests on Jupiter, Saturn, Uranus and Neptune 8.1 phase 11 · ready
  4. GEN.967 Dynamo field (b): the stored field strength, moment and lifetime come from the dynamo module and feed the radiation shielding 8.1 phase 11 · ready
  5. GEN.968 Neutron star structure (a): a TOV integration over the equation-of-state tables held on disk, tested against NICER radii 8.1 phase 11 · ready
  6. GEN.970 Stellar spectra (a): a spectral library entry (BT-Settl, MUSCLES for M dwarfs) and a lookup by temperature, gravity and activity, with tests on the Sun and an M dwarf 8.1 phase 11 · ready
  7. GEN.972 Climate feedback 1: ice-albedo on the surface temperature fixed point (ice cover grows as the planet cools and raises the albedo) 8.1 phase 11 · ready
  8. SEC.33 Release gate: a full security code audit of the whole code base, done before every major or minor version change (8.1 first) 8.1 phase 12 · ready
  9. TEST.261 Release gate: a full bug hunt audit of the whole code base and its behaviour, done before every major or minor version change (8.1 first) 8.1 phase 12 · ready
  10. TEST.300 End-to-end browser tests for the habitability system: symbols on lists, sector colouring and habitable-world markers 8.1 phase 5 · ready
  11. GEN.736 Magnetic field from a dynamo: the core's heat flow and rotation (buoyancy-flux scaling), reading the interior heat model 8.1 phase 11 · needs GEN.967
  12. GEN.743 Neutron star equation of state: integrate the tables for radius and structure, and add helium-core and oxygen-neon white dwarfs 8.1 phase 11 · needs GEN.968
  13. GEN.745 Stellar spectra by temperature, gravity and activity (model atmospheres) instead of a blackbody with a seeded UV share 8.1 phase 11 · needs GEN.970
  14. GEN.965 H-He equation of state tables (b): replace the n=1 polytrope in the giant interior solver with the tables 8.1 phase 11 · needs GEN.964
  15. GEN.974 Climate feedback 3: cloud albedo on the fixed point (cloud cover changes the reflected share) 8.1 phase 11 · needs GEN.972
  16. GEN.733 Hydrogen-helium equation-of-state tables for giant interiors: download job and use in the interior model 8.1 phase 11 · needs GEN.964, GEN.965
  17. GEN.752 Climate feedback on the surface temperature fixed point: ice-albedo, water vapour and cloud albedo 8.1 phase 11 · needs GEN.972, GEN.974
  18. GEN.301 Headline of 8.1: the new generate scatters, accurate galactic generation and scattering including the halo 8.1 phase 12 · needs SEC.33, TEST.261, TEST.300, GEN.733, GEN.736, GEN.743, GEN.744, GEN.745, GEN.752, GEN.753

Goals and their GitHub issues

71 open goal issues. Each groups the TODO items of one goal and is what we are working toward; a goal issue carries the status label "in progress" only while code for one of its items is being written, plus a dated comment with where it stands. Findings that belong to a goal (a failed test, a bug noticed in passing) are comments on its issue. Closed goals are on GitHub.

The five queues, in order, with each core's waits on another lane, are in /mnt/project-files/reports/8-1-core-queues-2026-10-22.md; a core takes its next item from the top of its own queue, and an item that is not in any queue waits for the TODO thread to place it. Epochs and Alembic revisions are numbered at merge, so two queues never claim one number. SEC.33 and TEST.261 get their own threads when PERF.146 closes, and GEN.301 closes last. Boss's live observations are bugs

Boss, 8:44 PM EDT 2026-10-22: everything he reports from live observation of the running app is a bug, not a feature or an enhancement. Each such report is filed as a bug item marked "(bug)", whatever its wording (the Generate form layout and naming notes, the menu bar changes, the plan warning and implausible plan numbers, a slow stage). Filed so far and re-kinded on this date: ADM.127 (Generate form), UX.146 (menu bar), GEN.625 (Auto defaults and the plan warning) and PERF.163 (the 264 s plan stage); the earlier live reports MAP.209, MAP.210, MAP.249 and TEST.281 were already bugs. The plan-numbers check Boss asked for (actual against expected density, the 157 globular clusters and 11 satellites) is filed as a bug when its read-only result arrives. UX.142 (viewing windows scale to the browser) was a design note for 8.2, not an observation, and stays a feature unless Boss says otherwise.

A lane that rebases onto main re-runs that test. It is a merge race the gate cannot see (two PRs, each green alone): Core 1 pointed this out after #2510, so a lane that adds a table also runs test_column_units.py against current main just before it merges.

Files two lanes both touch

  • Galaxy Map files and the star-budget ranking: Core 2 owns MAP.249 (browser frame time) (MAP.251 merged, #2375); MAP.239, MAP.243, MAP.247, MAP.250 and MAP.252 are merged. The star-budget code is _top_by_key in db/query.py; no other lane edits it.
  • The gates share four cores: a gate that sees load can fail slow browser tests (TEST.126, TEST.171, TEST.172, TEST.227); those are filed on goal #1694 and owned by Bug Hunt. A timed-out fuzz test can leave planetgen:run:active set (TEST.260); since #2345 a new run takes over from a dead holder, and python3 -m planetgen.cli.reset_jobs clears every run record by hand.
  • The 8.1 closing audits: TEST.185, TEST.184, TEST.159, TEST.160, GEN.301's closing and the full scripts/test.py full --check belong to Core 5 (coordinator, 4:59 PM EDT 2026-10-21), after PERF.121 and PERF.122 and before PERF.119. Order of the last stages (Boss, 6:34 PM EDT 2026-10-21; TODO thread, 6:40 PM EDT): these audits first, then the performance profile PERF.146 (a repeatable benchmark of every generation option and scatter, a report and a ranked optimization list), then the security audit SEC.33 and the bug hunt audit TEST.261 on the final code, then the headline GEN.301.
  • The release gates (Boss, 5:10 PM EDT 2026-10-21): before every minor and major stamp (8.1, 8.2 and on to 10.0; not the 8.0.NNNN builds; Decided, Boss, 9:03 PM EDT 2026-10-21, tapped "Every minor and major stamp") two standing audits must have a dated report for that version: SEC.33, the full security code audit (its own thread, started when the version nears close, never the lane that wrote the code), and TEST.261, the full bug hunt audit (Bug Hunt). The reports live in /mnt/project-files/reports/ as release-audit-security-<version>.md and release-audit-bug-hunt-<version>.md; a blocking finding stops the stamp. For 8.1 both gate GEN.301 and start after TEST.159 and TEST.160 merge. Each version page carries the same line in its closing phase.
  • A fresh lane machine: the dataset cache /var/cache/planetgen/datasets is empty until python3 -m planetgen.cli.datasets fetch runs (every plan or galaxy run stops with 'the downloaded data is missing'), and a state/maintenance.json left by a failed update refuses runs with UpdateRunning unless PLANETGEN_STATE_DIR points elsewhere (TEST.189). Test databases left by killed gates (planetgen_test_*) can fill the disk; drop them after each gate.
  • The gate environment (Core 1, about 5:43 AM and 6:07 AM EDT 2026-10-22 as the lane reported; the coordinator's relay): a gate needs PLANETGEN_TEST_REDIS_URL set beside the PLANETGEN_TEST_* MySQL variables (the coordinator is not certain whether PLANETGEN_MYSQL_* must also be unset; Core 1's wording is kept); with PLANETGEN_MYSQL_* exported and no test Redis URL a lane's gate shows about 200 false Busy reds (the first pass printed "failures: 8 test(s) failed; running each again alone" after 199 reds). With the right environment Core 1's full gate had 1 failure in 19,990 (TEST.267). A gate also needs PLANETGEN_TEST_* set in the same shell (source /etc/profile.d/planetgen-test.sh); otherwise the workers share one run lock and hundreds of tests fail with Busy (Core 3, relayed 8:44 AM EDT 2026-10-22). A test that launches a real planetgen run process on the default Redis prefix can collide with another worker's run on the machine's one live-run lock and fail Busy (TEST.266, fixed in #2466 with the fixture tests/private_redis.py, Bug Hunt, 9:04 AM EDT as the lane reported) (the real_queue tests were not changed). TEST.280 is fixed in #2573 (Bug Hunt, 10:11 PM EDT as the lane reported): conftest sets PLANETGEN_REDIS_URL to the Redis database numbered after the xdist worker, so every worker and every process its tests start uses its own database; tests/private_redis.py keeps only the URL helper and a flush, the TEST.266 fixture is gone, and the seven real_queue modules (test_api_jobs, test_inline_queue, test_reset_jobs, test_rq_time_limits, test_update_guard_work, test_wanted_workers_key, test_workers) need nothing. A lane machine's disk can fill up because the test MySQL writes binary logs by default (one lane held 25 GB); with under 5 GB free on the database drive a generate run refuses to start (PERF.3) and tests fail (TEST.268). Set skip-log-bin (or binlog_expire_logs_seconds=3600, with expire_logs_days=0) in the lane MySQL config, and run PURGE BINARY LOGS BEFORE NOW() if the lane's sandbox allows it (Bug Hunt, relayed 10:00 AM EDT 2026-10-22; Blocker 2's machine hit 25 GB of binary logs too, relayed 5:40 PM EDT as the lane's clock reads, and test_galaxy_gen refused runs with "leave 2.4 GB free"; the fix was PURGE BINARY LOGS BEFORE NOW() plus SET GLOBAL binlog_expire_logs_seconds=3600); TEST.268 is fixed in #2502 (Bug Hunt, 3:28 PM EDT as the lane reported: the suite now shows the code a 200 GB drive unless a test sets its own, so the five generate tests no longer depend on the host's free space), but a lane machine should still check df -h / and run PURGE BINARY LOGS BEFORE NOW(); SET PERSIST binlog_expire_logs_seconds=3600 (or set skip-log-bin), because a full drive still slows or stops the gate; Core 1's machine hit the same 25 GB of logs (relayed 1:03 PM EDT 2026-10-22) and failed test_generation_stats::test_estimate_only_writes_nothing, three test_galaxy_gen outside_the_outline tests and test_quiet_system_cost until it purged them. Kill a stale gate by PID (pattern scripts/test.py|pytest|planetgen.cli.worker), not by name (Core 3, relayed 10:43 AM EDT 2026-10-22). A lane MySQL with performance_schema OFF cannot run the gentle-wait test in test_drop_database_holders.py (another database's reader is never found, so it failed even alone on main); the test now skips when performance_schema is not ON (Core 5, PERF.121/122, #2441, 12:28 PM EDT as the lane reported), so a lane that wants the test to run sets performance_schema=ON in its MySQL config. A gate on a fresh machine can fail on a missing NLTK words corpus ("Resource words not found", Blocker 3, relayed 10:38 PM EDT 2026-10-22 as the lane reported): run nltk.download('words') with NLTK_ALLOW_PROXIED_URLOPEN=1. Judge a gate by the rerun-alone list, and do not file failures that come from a machine missing the grant or the Redis URL.
  • The database server: MySQL 8.0.19 or later only; MariaDB is not supported (Boss, 4:37 PM EDT 2026-10-21, DB.80). A lane machine that installed MariaDB fails the VALUES ROW tests and must run MySQL 8 instead.
  • A lane machine's MySQL setup: the MySQL user needs GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON planetgen%.* ... WITH GRANT OPTION (INSTALL.md shows the grant; a fresh machine's pgtest user without the grant option fails the same way, Core 1, about 6:07 AM EDT 2026-10-22 as the lane reported), or every test that creates a planetgen_test_* database fails with error 1044 (seen as test_update_database_step::test_a_failed_migration_stops_the_step_and_keeps_the_version on the Galaxy dials machine, about 10:20 PM EDT 2026-10-21 as the lane reported; a machine setup gap, it passes where the grant exists). A machine that runs the full gate in parallel also needs a larger innodb_redo_log_capacity, or tests lose the connection under load (TEST.262's third sighting). A lane must keep its turn open while a gate runs: the container restarts when a lane ends a turn during a gate, which kills the run (Blocker 2, about 4:02 AM EDT 2026-10-22 as the lane reported); wait on the process and its log inside the turn, and re-run a gate that was killed.
  • Global documents (Boss, 11:25 PM EDT): only the TODO thread regenerates the generated pages that span the project (the architecture map, docs/index.html, docs/plan/*.html); a lane's PR never touches or regenerates one, and a stale page must never fail a lane's gate. DOC.69 built the mechanism (Core 5, #2172, merged 12:58 AM EDT as the lane clock read): the freshness tests are marked globaldocs and skipped unless PLANETGEN_GLOBAL_DOCS=1 (scripts/test.py <command> --global-docs), and the TODO thread runs python scripts/build_global_docs.py (or --check) after lane merges.
  • Adding an /admin route (Admin redesign lane, relayed 2:41 PM EDT 2026-10-22 as the lane reported): run test_fuzz_web_routes, test_api_auth_sweep, test_web_old_urls_errors and test_web_adminregistry before the gate; a new route must be in the admin registry or its exempt list, and must answer a hostile query string fast (TEST.275).
  • The test container state: a test that runs migrate can leave state/maintenance.json set (TEST.189); a lane that finds it works round it with PLANETGEN_STATE_DIR.

Data tables carry no licence

Rule (Boss, 4:41 to 4:42 PM EDT 2026-10-22, relayed 4:42 PM EDT as the lane's clock reads): a data table has no licence column and no loader or test checks one. Licence information lives in one file in the repo root, DATA-LICENSE.md (Boss, 4:46 PM EDT 2026-10-22; the project's own LICENSE.md stays as it is, and GEN.623 creates the new file and removes the existing columns and checks from the gas table, the element table and the possibility-table loader). A lane that builds a new table (GEN.427, GEN.478, GEN.458, GEN.495) leaves the column out, and a lane with an open table that carries a licence field (Blocker 2, Core 4) drops it when GEN.623 merges. This does not touch source and citation fields: every number still states where it was read. GEN.623 is done: the column and checks went in #2580 (GEN.427) and the README pointer, the comet-source section of DATA-LICENSE.md and the stale licence sentences went in #2596 (GEN.495); TEST.214 must simply add no licence assertions.

Working while a gate runs

Boss, 7:41 PM EDT 2026-10-21: every lane builds the next steps of its build path in a separate worktree while its gate runs, and rebases that worktree as needed to stay current with main. Never edit the checkout a gate is running against (the gate must test the code it started on); a container restart still kills a gate, so a lane keeps its turn active and checks the process and log every 10 minutes.

Earlier decisions

Reordering decisions recorded 2026-10-09 to 2026-10-10 (history: the lane names and item states in it are out of date; the current plan is above)
  • Rush job (Boss, 2026-10-10 03:01Z and 03:04Z): GEN.185 (PR #953), GEN.184 (PR #965) and GEN.183 (PR #969) are merged. The mass cut slider has presets 8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 18 and 20 (the CLI refuses other values), the luminosity ladder runs 2500 to 4,000,000 L_sun with 3000 as the default, and the five passes are built. GEN.187 (back scatter by mass) has landed (PR #1059).
  • Progress bars (Boss, 2026-10-09 23:34Z and 23:38Z): one mechanism, bars start themselves on any sub-step predicted over 15 seconds, all in Bugfixes lane 1. Order: PERF.32 (the stats table), PERF.33 (the estimator), PERF.51 (the mechanism), then PERF.50 and DB.15, then UX.84 (bug) registers the rest of the sub-steps. Foundations lane 1 hands over any PERF.32 work; DB.21 (merged) read the estimator.
  • The fly-through Galaxy Map (MAP.146 to MAP.155) is in Phase 1 and is the headline of the 8.1 release (Boss, 2026-10-09 23:07Z). Phase 1 is complete only when these are done too, so 8.1 is stamped only then and the 8.0 hold stays until that day. It is Foundations 2 step 4; MAP.147 (the wire format) is done as an investigation and built as MAP.157 to MAP.159; ADM.29 (Foundations 1) must land before MAP.151.
  • Nothing is held (Boss, 2026-10-09 07:54Z), so the 108 open Phase 1 items are spread over Foundations 1 and 2 (50 and 41 list entries, some of them Phase 2), Bugfixes lane 1 (12) and Bugfixes lane 2 (14); TEST.139, the test suite rebuild, belongs to the Test Suite Rebuild thread, not a lane (NAV.47, NAV.48, TEST.125, UX.93 and the test failures the lanes reported; coordinator, 2026-10-10) (and MAP.147, the wire format record) are in the lane lists, unblockers first (DB.7, NAV.10, ADM.18, GEN.108 and GEN.107 have merged since). Within each lane the items that free the most other work come ahead of the leaves: GEN.84 (frees 17), OPS.8 (15), OPS.13 and GEN.58 (14), GEN.56 (14), DB.7 (13), NAV.10 (12), ADM.18 (10), GEN.106 (8). The habitability chain (GEN.84 to GEN.89, GEN.83) and the tech-level design (POP.8) now sit on Foundations 2 right behind GEN.56 and GEN.106; The internal same-seed items GEN.57 and TEST.77 stay last (GEN.59 was dropped with the user-facing rebuild, Boss 2026-10-09 20:42Z).
  • Orbital updates moves to the front, and GEN.106 stays on Foundations 2 right after GEN.56 (Boss's decision on the card). GEN.106 goes ahead of GEN.104 because it unblocks GEN.107, GEN.108, GEN.115, GEN.109 and GEN.105; GEN.104 depends on nothing. Boss moved every Foundations 3 item into Foundations 1 and 2, so GEN.104 and the phase 2 orbital chain (GEN.115, GEN.109, GEN.110, ADM.36, GEN.105) now run on Foundations 2 behind GEN.106. Everything after GEN.106 in this group is proposed, not yet approved.
  • GEN.56 is merged (PR #791). Every generation draw now goes through planetgen/util/draw.py, so GEN.127 is unblocked and GEN.57 can start, but it still goes last. Foundations 2 moves on to GEN.106.
  • New random draws use the helpers in util/draw.py. A test fails if a generator imports random, secrets or uuid directly, so GEN.98, GEN.100 and GEN.104 have to use them.
  • The map bugs go first in the Bugfix lane. MAP.135 to MAP.138 (all merged) touched the same map files as the Maps group, so they land before Foundations 2 starts Maps. Foundations 2 and the Bugfix lane never edit those files at the same time.
  • Nearby search and Routing go to Foundations 1 after GEN.100, in that order. GEN.100 is also the one outside blocker of NAV.47.
  • The small blockers of the internal same-seed work (DB.7, ADM.18, OPS.8, OPS.13, GEN.58 and OPS.14 are all merged) were filler work. They depend on nothing and take little time, so any lane that has a gap takes them. They are what keeps GEN.55 from queuing behind them later.
  • NAV.12 and GEN.84 do not hang off seeded draws. NAV.12 only reads stored data, and GEN.84 is a design note with no draws. The items that do depend on the draws are the generation items that add them: GEN.104, GEN.85 to GEN.88, GEN.99, GEN.101, GEN.127 and GEN.128 to GEN.130.
